Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The molecular_biology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 663. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 840 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 663 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the molecular_biology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 840. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
